Urban buildings can only be constructed in urban locations.

## Town buildings

The following buildings can only be constructed in locations with the [![Town.png](/thumb.php?f=Town.png&width=24)](/Town "Town") Town, [![City.png](/thumb.php?f=City.png&width=24)](/City "City") City *or* [![Megalopolis.png](/thumb.php?f=Megalopolis.png&width=24)](/Megalopolis "Megalopolis") Megalopolis rank and are removed if the location rank is decreased.

### Basic Industry

[![icon](/thumb.php?f=Building_category_basic_industry_category.png&width=64)](/File%3ABuilding_category_basic_industry_category.png "icon")

These are the [Buildings](/Buildings "Buildings") that create [Goods](/Goods "Goods") that are primarily used by other [Buildings](/Buildings "Buildings").

| [![Gymnasium icon](/thumb.php?f=Building_gymnasium_building.png&width=64)](/File%3ABuilding_gymnasium_building.png "Gymnasium icon") Gymnasium  To ensure the continued prosperity of our government, we shall establish a new system of education for our elite. With a newly established separate institution, we can ensure access to the highest quality tutors while keeping their efforts centered on those who deserve it the most. ## City buildings

The following buildings can only be constructed in locations with the [![City.png](/thumb.php?f=City.png&width=24)](/City "City") City *or* [![Megalopolis.png](/thumb.php?f=Megalopolis.png&width=24)](/Megalopolis "Megalopolis") Megalopolis rank and are removed if the location rank is decreased.

### Colonial Buildings

[![icon](/thumb.php?f=Building_category_colonial_category.png&width=64)](/File%3ABuilding_category_colonial_category.png "icon")

Across the city, we are establishing a secret workshop in the Palazzo Vecchio, where alchemists and craftsmen perfect a soft-paste porcelain — the first produced in Europe, rivalling the precious wares imported at great expense from China. | [![Ribeira das Naus icon](/thumb.php?f=Building_ribeira_das_naus.png&width=64)](/File%3ABuilding_ribeira_das_naus.png "Ribeira das Naus icon") Ribeira das Naus  The Navy Arsenal site, formerly a medieval naval dockyard called *tercenas*, witnessed a shift during King D. Manuel I's construction of the Paço da Ribeira. The establishment of new tercenas, known as *Ribeira das Naus*, to the west of the royal palace, played a crucial role in building ships for the Portuguese Discoveries. These shipyards, the largest in the Portuguese Empire, served as a model for overseas establishments, notably in Goa and Cochin. | * [![Max ships built at same time.png](/thumb.php?f=Max_ships_built_at_same_time.png&width=32)](/File%3AMax_ships_built_at_same_time.png) **+2** Ship-building Capacity * [![Local sailors.png](/thumb.php?f=Local_sailors.png&width=32)](/File%3ALocal_sailors.png) **+0.02** Monthly Sailors * [![Can build ships in this location positive.png](/thumb.php?f=Can_build_ships_in_this_location_positive.png&width=32)](/File%3ACan_build_ships_in_this_location_positive.png) Can Build Ships Here: **yes** * [![Local repair speed.png](/thumb.php?f=Local_repair_speed.png&width=32)](/File%3ALocal_repair_speed.png) **+2.00%** Navy Repair Speed | **Location:**  * always: False   **Country:**   * always: False | 90 | * [![Masonry](/thumb.php?f=Goods_masonry.png&width=32)](/Goods#Masonry "Masonry") **0.1** Masonry * [![Naval Supplies](/thumb.php?f=Goods_naval_supplies.png&width=32)](/Goods#Naval_Supplies "Naval Supplies") **0.2** Naval Supplies * [![Tools](/thumb.php?f=Goods_tools.png&width=32)](/Goods#Tools "Tools") **0.2** Tools * [![Lumber](/thumb.php?f=Goods_lumber.png&width=32)](/Goods#Lumber "Lumber") **1.0** Lumber | 2000 [![Burghers](/thumb.php?f=Pop_burghers.png&width=32)](/Pop_type#Burghers "Burghers") | 1 | * Ribeira das Naus   + Input:     - [![Tar](/thumb.php?f=Goods_tar.png&width=32)](/Goods#Tar "Tar") **0.25** Tar     - [![Naval Supplies](/thumb.php?f=Goods_naval_supplies.png&width=32)](/Goods#Naval_Supplies "Naval Supplies") **0.8** Naval Supplies     - [![Tools](/thumb.php?f=Goods_tools.png&width=32)](/Goods#Tools "Tools") **0.15** Tools     - [![Lumber](/thumb.php?f=Goods_lumber.png&width=32)](/Goods#Lumber "Lumber") **0.2** Lumber     - [![Stone](/thumb.php?f=Goods_stone.png&width=32)](/Goods#Stone "Stone") **0.1** Stone |  |
